   59|  2.35k|    pub(crate) fn push(
   60|  2.35k|        &mut self,
   61|  2.35k|        k: [f64; 3],

   63|  2.35k|        b: [Complex64; 3],
   64|  2.35k|        weight: f64,
   65|  2.35k|    ) -> Result<(), SolverError> {

   78|  2.35k|        ];
